While prior work has made progress in measuring and mitigating bias, it largely focuses on final outputs of models, with limited understanding of the mechanisms that produce biased outcomes. Recent advances in LLM reasoning offers a new lens for investigating bias, yet the link between reasoning and bias remains poorly understood. Existing approaches focus primarily on final answer correctness or explicitly biased language, overlooking different behaviours in reasoning that can drive biased outcomes. We introduce BiasTrace, an annotation scheme for labelling reasoning behaviours in model-generated traces and linking them to biased outcomes. BiasTrace captures bias-specific behaviours (e.g., unsupported demographic assumptions) as well as general reasoning patterns that may implicitly contribute to bias (e.g. overthinking). We apply BiasTrace to reasoning traces in bias-sensitive contexts, scaled using validated LLM-as-a-judge methods, producing a large annotated dataset. Our analysis shows that biased outputs often stem from subtle reasoning behaviours rather than explicitly biased language, and that reasoning-level annotations improve bias detection. We further show that BiasTrace behaviours can be exploited for inference-time mitigation. These findings underscore the importance of examining a broader range of reasoning patterns to better understand bias in LLMs.

</details>


### [124] [FreeBalance: Pre-Routing Online Moe Load Balancing via Residual Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14205)
*Pengfei Chen,Yize Wu,Shouxu Kuang,Ke Gao,Ling Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FreeBalance，一种通过残差工作负载预测将专家迁移与前置计算阶段重叠的无损在线负载均衡框架，可降低最大-平均秩负载比32.8%，端到端预填充延迟13.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型分布式推理中专家并行面临负载不均问题，现有在线均衡依赖路由后统计，将迁移开销置于关键路径上，导致延迟增加。

Method: 利用残差网络中隐藏表示的跨层相似性构建轻量级工作负载预测器，在路由决策前预判路由分布并提前规划专家迁移，同时通过成本模型限制交换次数以隐藏同步开销。

Result: 在多种模型和数据集上，最大-平均秩负载比降低32.8%，端到端预填充延迟降低13.1%，平均每层隐藏5.1个专家的均衡开销，约占关键路径延迟的8.5%。

Conclusion: FreeBalance通过预测与重叠机制有效消除在线负载均衡的同步开销，实现无损且高效的MoE推理负载均衡。

Abstract: Load imbalance poses a major bottleneck to the efficiency of expert parallelism in distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. The most heavily loaded rank stalls global execution due to skewed routing distributions, directly increasing latency. While offline expert placement can alleviate persistent imbalance, practical multi-task serving workloads exhibit layer- and batch-dependent routing dynamics, making online load balancing indispensable. Existing approaches rely on routing statistics collected after each MoE router, requiring expert weight load or migration to begin only after routing decisions are available, consequently placing migration overhead on the inference critical path. In this work, we observe that online balancing can instead be largely overlapped with computation before target routing (e.g., attention), if routing distributions can be predicted accurately in advance. Therefore, we propose FreeBalance, a lossless online load-balancing framework that overlaps expert migration with preceding computation stages via residual workload prediction. FreeBalance leverages cross-layer similarities in hidden representations within the residual network to build a lightweight workload predictor. This enables proactive expert migration planning before routing decisions are available, creating substantial overlap between weight transfer and computation-heavy pre-routing stages. Furthermore, a cost model constrains the number of swaps to fully hide the synchronization overhead within the available window. Experiments across models and datasets show that FreeBalance reduces the max-to-mean rank load ratio by 32.8% and end-to-end prefill latency by 13.1%. Specifically, our method hides balancing overhead of an average of 5.1 experts per layer, which would otherwise account for about 8.5% of the critical-path latency.

</details>


### [125] [APTER: Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14212)
*Xukai Wang,Liangqi Li,Zhiyue Xu,Jingang Zhou,Xiaoyu Shi,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出APTTER框架，将专家构建的领域标准转化为查询级细粒度评分标准，用于专业复杂推理的后训练优化与能力诊断，在数学和医学任务上平均提升最高达15.86和8.04分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法依赖整体偏好或结果验证，基于评分标准的方法常为每个查询独立生成标准，在专业领域可能遗漏关键要求且跨样本不一致，难以诊断和修复持续的能力缺陷。

Method: 从领域专家构建的标准体系出发，为每个查询选择相关标准并实例化为查询级评分标准（链接到来源标准）；然后利用评分结论同时作为优化信号和标准级诊断信号，聚合低分结论按标准ID识别持久缺陷，在强化学习期间触发定向监督微调更新。

Result: 在数学推理和医学问答两个领域、三代模型上均取得一致提升，数学和医学平均分相对基础模型最高提升15.86和8.04分。

Conclusion: APTTER通过专家锚定的结构化评分标准，实现了可解释的细粒度诊断和针对性修复，有效提升专业领域的复杂推理能力，且代码和评分数据集已公开。

Abstract: As large language models enter professional domains, they must satisfy domain constraints, include critical evidence, and provide complete reasoning rather than merely produce fluent responses. Existing post-training methods often rely on holistic preferences or outcome-level verification, while recent rubric-based methods usually generate rubrics independently for each query. In specialized domains, such unconstrained rubrics may omit critical requirements and vary across samples, hindering the diagnosis and targeted repair of persistent capability deficiencies. We propose APTER (Adaptive Post-Training with Expert-Grounded Rubrics), a framework that integrates structured domain knowledge into fine-grained evaluation, optimization, and diagnosis for specialized complex reasoning. First, expert-grounded rubric construction starts from an expert criteria framework built by domain experts, where each criterion represents a stable professional capability. For each query, APTER selects relevant criteria and instantiates them into query-level rubrics linked to their source criteria, turning reusable expert criteria into executable query-level supervision without reference answers. Second, adaptive post-training uses rubric verdicts as both optimization and criterion-level diagnostic signals. Aggregating low-scoring verdicts by criterion ID reveals persistent deficiencies and triggers targeted supervised fine-tuning updates during reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and medical question answering show consistent gains across both domains. Across three model generations, APTER improves the mathematics and medical averages over the corresponding base models by up to 15.86 and 8.04 points, respectively. Code and rubric datasets are available at https://github.com/AntDT-APTER/APTER.

</details>


### [126] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DeaMoE架构，通过专家分组共享参数和两阶段路由策略，减少解码时权重加载量，提升MoE模型小批量解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在实时交互场景下使用小批量解码时受内存带宽限制，专家权重加载成为瓶颈，现有方法（压缩、细粒度专家设计）有精度损失或额外开销。

Method: 将专家按专业领域分组为部门，同部门专家共享大部分参数，各保留少量私有参数；设计定制两阶段路由策略避免冗余加载。

Result: 相比vanilla MoE，每步权重加载减少50.9%，7B模型端到端TPOT提速1.33倍，DeepSeek-V3微基准在A40和H100上峰值提速2.00倍和1.97倍。

Conclusion: DeaMoE有效缓解MoE解码内存瓶颈，在保持精度的同时显著提升解码效率，适用于低延迟交互场景。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [127] [MathForm: Scaling Mathematical Autoformalization with Knowledge Retrieval and Verification-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14221)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hengyu Zhao,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MathForm框架，通过Mathlib知识检索和验证引导的迭代修订构建约367K条Lean 4形式化数据，训练MathForm-8B模型，在六项基准上优于多个32B专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化方法依赖模型参数记忆库知识且缺乏反馈修订机制，难以处理数学概念到形式库类型/定义的复杂映射，导致生成语句保真度不足。

Method: 先由检索规划器从Mathlib获取相关定义及既有形式化示例，随后生成器产出候选语句，再结合编译器诊断与语义一致性反馈进行迭代修订，最终通过监督微调与强化学习训练模型。

Result: MathForm-8B在六项基准上平均Pass@8率达88.06%（语法检查）和72.37%（一致性检查），在FATE-H/FATE-X子集上一致性通过率分别达63%和37%，均超过最强专用基线。

Conclusion: 知识检索与验证引导的修订能有效提升自动形式化的准确性与库一致性，且小规模模型可在该框架下超越更大专用模型，展示了高质量数据构建对形式化任务的关键价值。

Abstract: Autoformalization is commonly framed as translating natural-language mathematical statements into machine-verifiable formal languages such as Lean 4. However, faithful formalization requires more than translation. Models must map mathematical concepts to the complex hierarchy of types and definitions in formal libraries such as Mathlib, while ensuring that generated statements preserve the meaning of the source propositions. Existing approaches struggle because they rely heavily on the model's parametric memory for library-specific knowledge, while common data construction pipelines often resort to filtering single-pass outputs and lack mechanisms for feedback-driven revision. To address these challenges, we introduce MathForm, an autoformalization framework for constructing verified training data through Mathlib knowledge retrieval and verification-guided iterative refinement. Before generation, a retrieval planner gathers relevant definitions and existing formalizations from Mathlib to guide the formalization generator. Generated statements are then revised using compiler diagnostics and semantic-consistency feedback. Using this framework, we construct FormalVerse, a Lean 4 dataset containing approximately 367K verified examples across diverse mathematical domains and sources. We then train MathForm-8B through supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning. Across six benchmarks, MathForm-8B achieves average Pass@8 rates of 88.06% under Syntax Check (SC) and 72.37% under Consistency Check (CC), outperforming multiple specialized 32B autoformalizers. On the challenging FATE-H and FATE-X subsets, it attains CC pass rates of 63% and 37%, exceeding the strongest specialized baselines in both cases.

</details>


### [128] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文发现Power Sampling在推理时虽能强化正确轨迹概率，却可能使下游推理性能下降，并提出了基于形变控制和支持保持的修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling有望作为通用前端提升推理，但研究发现其可能加剧概率集中而损害自一致性等下游方法，需厘清矛盾根源。

Method: 通过诊断剂量失配与覆盖失配，用形变控制且支持保持的Power目标替代全局均匀指数化，校准跨问题锐化并保留中等概率路径。

Result: 在同等预算下，修复后的采样器配合加权自一致性，逆转了全局Power导致的性能损失，并在推理基准上优于标准多样本推理，原准确性下降高达18.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 全局Power Sampling的锐化方式与下游推理需求不匹配，需进行问题自适应且支持保持的调整，才能发挥其增益潜力。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [129] [Polaris : Multi Agentic System for Conversational Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14246)
*Varuni H K,Soham Sarkar,Jay Kumar,Goutham Krishnan,Tanvi Johari,Avinash Bharadwaj,Santosh Hegde*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Polaris，一个监督式多智能体框架，通过动态任务协调（DTC）和ReAct风格代理，将自然语言查询转化为端到端的企业分析工作流，实现数据检索、可视化和推理解释，在结构化数据集上验证了高语义保真度和答案相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织面临数据丰富但洞察匮乏的困境，传统查询与解释企业级信息复杂耗时，亟需一种能快速访问、理解并行动于数据的对话式分析方案。

Method: 采用监督式多智能体架构，核心为动态任务协调（DTC）层，将代理-任务分配建模为自适应二分图匹配，结合reason-first的ReAct风格代理，实现实时协调、恢复与优化，从而构建连贯的分析工作流。

Result: 在结构化企业数据集上的评估显示，该方法达到高语义保真度和答案相关性，验证了多智能体编排的效能。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排有望提供可信赖、端到端的大规模商业智能，弥合数据与洞察之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: In today's fast-paced environment, the ability to swiftly access, understand, and act on data is no longer optional; it is essential. Yet most organizations remain data-rich but insight-poor, constrained by the complexity of querying, interpreting, and explaining enterprise-scale information. We present Polaris, a supervisor-led multi-agent framework for conversational enterprise analytics that bridges this gap. Polaris introduces Dynamic Task Coordination (DTC), a decision-theoretic orchestration layer that models agent-task assignment as adaptive bipartite matching, enabling real-time coordination, recovery, and optimization across specialized agents for querying, visualization, and reasoning. By coupling DTC with reason-first, ReAct-style agents, Polaris transforms natural-language queries into coherent analytical workflows that not only retrieve and visualize data but also explain the underlying "why." Evaluation on structured enterprise datasets demonstrates high semantic fidelity and answer relevancy, underscoring the potential of multi-agent orchestration to deliver trustworthy, end-to-end business intelligence at scale.

</details>


### [130] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于线性规划的NAS框架LP-NAS，利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构造LP以更新架构，提升泛化与效率，并在DARTS空间上实现S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS，实验证明其收敛快、精度高且可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微NAS将搜索空间松弛为连续域，可利用连续优化原理，但现有方法在架构更新方向的计算上未充分结合泛化与参数最优性，导致搜索效率与性能受限，亟需更高效的数学规划框架。

Method: LP-NAS将架构更新建模为线性规划：使用验证损失梯度驱动泛化改进，训练损失Hessian保持参数最优性，求解LP获得下降方向；提出两种计算高效变体S-LP（简化）和R-LP（正则化），并应用于DARTS搜索空间。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100上，LP-DARTS在搜索早期收敛更快、验证性能更高，搜索与评估阶段均优于标准DARTS；在CIFAR-10上超越P-DARTS、PC-DARTS和STO-DARTS；在ImageNet上验证了架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 数学规划（LP）为可微NAS提供了一种高效且稳健的优化途径，LP-NAS在收敛速度、性能和迁移性上均表现出优势，具有广泛适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [131] [TimeSage-EV: A Live Benchmark for Agentic Time Series Analysis in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14270)
*Qingren Yao,Yaxuan Kong,Yuqi Nie,Yichen Li,Stefan Zohren,Anna Vettoruzzo,Qingsong Wen,Ming Jin,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出TimeSage-EV，首个面向动态数据发布的实时时间序列分析基准，涵盖60个真实机构场景和1485个问答对，评估LLM智能体的状态识别、数据总结与展望推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列QA基准依赖固定快照，无法评估模型在数据更新后的时间有效性和截止感知能力，而高 stakes 场景中新数据可能改变结论。

Method: 构建跨6领域、4种发布频率的实时基准，智能体每期接收数据与报告，用 withheld 目标发布作为真值；同时提出TimeSage-1.0，具备可复用分析技能库的自进化智能体。

Result: 前沿LLM在不同模型层级间表现差距显著，常见失败模式包括时间有效性错误、外生上下文使用不足和适应能力弱。

Conclusion: TimeSage-EV揭示动态环境下时间序列分析的关键挑战，提供月度更新、代码和排行榜作为持续研究资源。

Abstract: Time series analysis in high-stakes domains relies on recurring data releases, where new observations can alter the evidence base and the validity of later conclusions. Existing time series QA benchmarks mostly rely on fixed snapshots, leaving temporal validity and cutoff-aware evidence use unevaluated. We introduce TimeSage-EV, a live benchmark for agentic time series analysis in evolving environments. It tracks 60 real institutional scenarios across 6 domains, comprising 1,485 scenario-period QA pairs from Feb 2023 to May 2026 and spanning monthly, weekly, daily, and irregular release cadences. At each period, large language model (LLM) agents receive time series data and source reports, while the withheld target release provides ground truth. TimeSage-EV evaluates state identification, data summarization, and outlook reasoning. Experiments with frontier LLM agents and TimeSage-1.0, a novel self-evolving agent with a reusable analytical skill library, reveal significant performance gaps across model tiers and recurring failures in temporal validity, exogenous context use, and adaptation. We release TimeSage-EV as a research resource with monthly updates, code, a leaderboard, and failure-mode analyses.

</details>


### [132] [Intern-S2-Mobius: Foundation Model with Decoupled Knowledge and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14290)
*Kai Chen,Jifeng Ding,Ning Ding,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yicheng Gu,Qipeng Guo,Ermo Hua,Haian Huang,Haozheng Hou,Jie Hou,Xiangyu Hong,Che Jiang,Minxi Jin,Cheng Liang,Dahua Lin,Dawei Liu,Kuikun Liu,Chengqi Lv,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Ningsheng Ma,Biqing Qi,Jianmin Qian,Shiya Su,Youbang Sun,Huanze Tang,Zhongbo Tian,Hanjing Wang,Rui Wang,Ting Wang,Yi Wang,Baiting Wu,Jun Xu,Bowen Yang,Hui Wang,Weida Wang,Haochen Ye,Jiashuo Yu,Shan Yu,Xiaoyi Yu,Qirui Zeng,Qi Zhang,Ming Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Bowen Zhou,Xinyu Zhou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Mobius-v0架构，通过全局共享知识存储FFN与多个推理器Self-Attn分离，实现知识压缩与高效组合推理，在7B模型和35B模型上分别显著降低训练数据需求并提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构将知识存储与推理运算耦合，导致知识冗余、内存占用高且推理效率低，亟需一种知识-推理分离的架构来提升缩放效率与端侧部署性能。

Method: 将FFN作为全局共享的知识记忆库（存储知识向量），多个Self-Attn作为推理器，以隐藏状态为缓存和载体迭代查询所需知识并回传推理算子，形成知识-推理分离的回环结构。

Result: 1）7B版本从零训练，仅用基线62.6%的数据即达相似下游分数；2）基于Qwen3.5-35B持续预训练的Intern-S2-Mobius，在保持下游性能的同时实现近4倍端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: 知识-推理分离的架构能有效提升知识压缩率与推理效率，为大规模模型的高效训练与部署提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce Mobius-v0, an architecture that comprises a globally shared Memory (FFN) that stores knowledge vectors and multiple Reasoners (Self-Attn) that iteratively achieve compositional reasoning. Using hidden states as cache and carrier, reasoners repeatedly query memory for required knowledge-vectors, while the knowledge is transmitted back to reasoning operators. Through this knowledge-reasoning-separation architecture, Mobius achieves better knowledge compression and reasoning efficiency. Built upon Mobius-v0 architecture: 1) Our 7B model trained-from-scratch achieves similar downstream score as a 7B Transformer baseline with 62.6% of baseline's training data. 2) Our Intern-S2-Mobius, continually-pretrained from Qwen3.5-35B, achieves similar downstream score while delivering nearly 4x end-to-end inference speedup.

</details>


### [133] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Rollplex运行时，通过将参考/训练阶段的前缀计算重叠到 rollout 解码窗口，并利用相位感知内存管理与并行感知权重共享，在保持同步RL语义的同时显著提升VLM的RL后训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM的RL后训练采用严格的串行阶段（rollout、参考评分、actor训练），但VLM处理密集视频和长前缀的计算量大且与生成响应无关，导致GPU利用率不足。若能并行执行前缀计算与解码，可在不破坏同步策略更新的前提下提升吞吐。

Method: 设计Rollplex：1）分解参考和训练阶段，将前缀计算调度到 rollout 解码窗口内；2）相位感知内存管理按生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留；3）并行感知权重共享在不同张量并行度间复用布局兼容的物理存储，仅重建不兼容张量，避免完整第二份actor副本。

Result: 在32张H800 GPU上，相较于串行共置获得1.23×–1.30×加速，相较于相同GPU预算下的分离式部署获得1.57×–2.24×加速，同时保持同步RL更新语义。

Conclusion: Rollplex有效解决了VLM RL后训练中前缀计算与解码串行导致的资源浪费问题，通过智能调度与内存/权重复用，在不改变RL语义的前提下显著提升系统效率，适合大规模VLM训练场景。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [134] [Program-space Diffusion for Morphology-to-Transcriptomics Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14330)
*Ruyter Swann,Dorent Reuben,Racoceanu Daniel*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 将形态学到转录组的预测重构为在转录程序空间中的条件生成，利用cNMF提取低维转录程序并训练条件扩散模型，以降低任务维度并利用协调的转录变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高且可扩展性有限，现有基因级预测方法未利用转录组建模实践且基因选择策略不统一，导致公平比较困难。

Method: 使用共识非负矩阵分解（cNMF）从训练数据中提取低维转录程序，并训练条件扩散模型，根据组织学图像生成程序激活值，而非独立预测基因。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体数值或对比结果。

Conclusion: 该公式利用了协调的转录变异，并显著降低了条件生成任务的维度。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables genome-wide gene expression profiling while preserving tissue architecture, but its cost and limited scalability remain major bottlenecks. This has motivated models that predict spatial expression directly from routine histology. Despite promising results, most existing approaches operate at the gene level without leveraging established transcriptomic modeling practices and rely on heterogeneous gene selection strategies, which complicates fair comparison across methods.
  We propose to reformulate morphology-to-transcriptomics prediction as conditional generation in transcriptional program space, thereby exploiting coordinated transcriptional variation instead of predicting genes independently. Using consensus non-negative matrix factorization (cNMF), we extract a low-dimensional set of transcriptional programs capturing coordinated expression variation in the training data, and train a conditional diffusion model to generate program activations from histology. This formulation exploits coordinated transcriptional variation and substantially lowers the dimensionality of the conditional generative task.

</details>


### [135] [Wrong but Useful: Trajectory Value Beyond Answer Correctness in Multi-Agent Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14375)
*Chih-Hsuan Yang,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Cheng-Hau Yang,Weijian Zheng,Fernando Llorente,Xiaolong Ma,Xinyang Li,Eliu A. Huerta,Ian T. Foster,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过DHD协议发现，错误答案中常包含对后续推理有益的轨迹价值，正确性不能决定消息的保留价值，DHD可生成可重用的标签供智能体学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统基于正确性、置信度或自动评分过滤消息，但忽略错误消息可能携带有效的分解、约束或科学原理，这些轨迹价值未被测量。

Method: 提出DHD协议，缓存五条独立生成的消息并重放给同一积分器，对比每条消息可见与隐藏时的推理结果，量化其轨迹价值；结合对照重复实验和干预分析验证稳健性。

Result: 在五个数学科学基准和两个模型系列中均出现错误但有益的消息；每模型中超过四成的正确性改变消息为有益；重复性效应显著（p=0.0002）；完整消息效果最佳，推理比答案更关键。

Conclusion: 答案正确性不能决定轨迹价值，DHD能测量该缺失属性，并为智能体提供何时应采纳消息的可学习标签。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning systems often use agreement, confidence, or automated scores to decide which messages should shape a final answer. Such filtering assumes that a message likely to be correct is also worth keeping. Yet a wrong answer can contain a useful decomposition, constraint, or scientific principle. We test this distinction with Diverse Hypothesis Deliberation (DHD), a controlled measurement protocol that caches five independently generated messages and replays the same downstream solver, called the integrator, with each message available or hidden. The replay comparison measures a message's trajectory value: whether making the message available helps or harms subsequent reasoning. Across five mathematics and science benchmarks and two openly available model families, gpt-oss-120b and gemma-4-31B-it, wrong-helpful messages appear in every benchmark-model combination. Among wrong-answer messages that change final correctness, more than four in ten changes are helpful in each model. Controlled repeats show that the number of repeatable message effects is unlikely to arise from replay variation alone (p=0.0002). A focused intervention on repeatable wrong-helpful messages finds that the complete message works best, while retaining its reasoning preserves more success than retaining only its answer; the source of the complete-message advantage remains open. Within the same problem, repeated trajectory-value evidence also identifies a better keep-or-remove choice than answer correctness alone. Answer correctness is therefore informative but does not determine trajectory value. DHD measures this missing property and produces reusable labels for learning when agents should listen.

</details>


### [136] [LLMs Don't Pay for the Jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14397)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出机器归纳/演绎能力虽强，但缺乏使认知错误产生物理代价的机制，因此无法实现真正的溯因跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点将大语言模型的溯因局限归因于缺乏具身模拟，但普朗克黑体辐射案例表明无需具身也可完成溯因，促使作者寻找更根本的缺失要素。

Method: 通过分析普朗克假说与经典理论的内在矛盾，形式化引入‘热力学耦合’概念，并对比固定权重Transformer的推理特性，结合熵与准确率的实证数据。

Result: 固定权重Transformer推理缺乏认知错误与物理成本的耦合，其输出熵不随任务因果难度增加而显著变化，准确率却大幅下降。

Conclusion: 机器溯因的缺失关键可能比具身更根本：需要系统具备某种物理机制使认知错误产生足够的代价，从而强制修正假设。

Abstract: Zahavy [2026] argues that Large Language Models, despite their capabilities in induction and deduction, cannot perform the abductive "Jump" that produced Einstein's equivalence principle, and attributes this limitation to the absence of embodied simulation. Zheng-Xin [2026] and Farmer [2026] question whether embodiment is necessary for abduction, pointing to alternative routes to General Relativity and forms of abduction that require no sensorimotor grounding. Max Planck resolved the blackbody radiation problem in 1900. Planck's move to E = hν required no embodied simulation. It was motivated by a mathematical consequence of classical theory, an infinite predicted energy for a finite measured quantity, that could not be physically accepted. We show that neither induction nor deduction could have produced the postulate and argue that its adoption required a coupling between epistemic error and physical cost. We formalize this distinction through thermodynamic coupling and show that fixed-weight transformer inference lacks such coupling, regardless of model scale. This is consistent with empirical results showing that output entropy remains nearly unchanged across tasks with sharply increasing causal difficulty, even as accuracy falls from 100% to 17%. We therefore argue that the missing ingredient in machine abduction may lie deeper than embodiment: a system must have some physical mechanism through which epistemic error becomes costly enough to force revision.

</details>


### [137] [The Past and Future of AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14407)
*Ross D. King*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI科学家的过去与未来，指出自动化科学的关键在于整合神经学习、逻辑、概率、数学、因果推理、模拟、实验设计、机器人与正式科学记录，并展望其变革科学、加速发现的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Adam和Eve等早期系统的出现，以及基础模型、自主智能体和实验室机器人的发展，科学自动化已从单一组件可行转向整体集成挑战；作者旨在系统梳理进展，并强调集成是当前核心问题。

Method: 通过回顾Adam和Eve的里程碑贡献，分析现代AI科学家所需的架构要素，提出整合多学科方法（如神经学习、因果推理、机器人实验等）的集成框架，并以诺贝尔图灵挑战为远景目标。

Result: 指出2025年前进展超前于计划，AI科学家已能实现假设生成、实验设计到结果解释的循环，且具备处理超复杂系统、大规模协作等能力，但尚未达到完全自主通用水平。

Conclusion: AI科学家成功将创造新型科学范式并深刻改变世界，其核心突破取决于跨组件无缝集成，而非单点技术提升。

Abstract: We present a survey of the past and future of AI Scientists: machines capable of automating science. AI Scientists can originate hypotheses, deduce their consequences, design and execute experiments, interpret their results, and revise their beliefs. Such systems are integrated scientific agents, connected to the literature, formal knowledge, mathematical models, simulations, data-analysis systems and physical laboratories.
  Adam was the first machine to make novel scientific discoveries through cycles of hypothesis formation and physical experimentation. Eve established the architecture of the modern self-driving laboratory. Foundation models, autonomous agents and laboratory robotics now make it possible to build systems far more general than either Adam or Eve.
  The central problem is no longer whether individual components of science can be automated. They can. The problem is integration. AI Scientists must combine neural learning with logic, probability, mathematics, causal reasoning, simulation, experimental design, robotics and formal scientific records.
  AI Scientists have the potential to transform science: to make science faster, cheaper, more systematic and more reproducible. AI Scientists could investigate systems too complicated for unaided human science, and enable thousands of AI scientists to work together on single problems.
  The Nobel Turing Challenge sets the goal of developing by 2050 AI systems capable of automating Nobel-quality discoveries. Progress is ahead of schedule. When we succeed it will create a new form of science and transform the world.

</details>


### [138] [Knowing When to Stop: Bayesian Optimal Stopping for LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14425)
*Toby D. Pilditch*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出自适应停止框架optstop，通过贝叶斯序贯测量在不确定性高时继续采样、精准时停止，可减少57%-97%的试验次数且结论等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定采样预算在估计已精确时仍重复测试，浪费算力，无法根据不确定性动态分配资源。

Method: 基于分层贝叶斯推断的序贯决策框架，支持二值/序数/连续结果，无需校准题库，可在线或回溯运行，并含低性能下谨慎采样的安全机制。

Result: 在200项×10轮评估的9种验证设置中减少57%-97%计划试验，整体结论与完整运行一致。

Conclusion: LLM评估算力可按不确定性而非固定重复次数分配，节省幅度取决于评估设计。

Abstract: LLM evaluations often use fixed sampling budgets, testing every item the same number of times even after estimates are precise. We introduce optstop, a precision-based adaptive stopping framework that treats evaluation as a sequential measurement problem: keep sampling where uncertainty remains high, and stop where estimates are precise or stable enough. The framework builds on hierarchical Bayesian inference, supports binary, ordinal, and continuous outcomes, and keeps every benchmark item eligible for sampling, without requiring a calibrated item bank. It runs live or retrospectively, and includes a safeguard that samples more cautiously as measured performance approaches zero, where rare successes matter most. In an illustrative 200-item, 10-epoch evaluation, it removes 57%-97% of planned trials across nine validation settings, with overall conclusions equivalent to the full run. These results show that LLM evaluation compute can be allocated by uncertainty rather than by fixed repetition counts, with the magnitude of savings depending on evaluation design.

</details>


### [139] [PACE-Bench: Benchmarking Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14441)
*Yuhao Zhan,Bingxiang He,Zecong Tang,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出PACE-Bench基准，包含144个物理领域源到目标环境适配对，用于评估自进化智能体在环境变化后的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体评估在固定执行条件下进行，未测试环境变化后的恢复能力，缺乏对物理条件变化下智能体适应性的系统评估。

Method: 构建模拟器基准PACE-Bench，涵盖6个物理域144个适配对；智能体需在有限尝试预算内，利用诊断性沙盒反馈，将源环境代码设计迭代适配到目标环境。

Result: 对比10种方法，性能未饱和：Reflexion+Qwen3-14B仅35.9%成功率，GPT-5.5在Statics子集达66.7%；模拟器反馈优于无验证自我修订，记忆和树搜索效果有限；即使暴露物理变化也不能提高上限。

Conclusion: 模拟器接地反馈是实现自适应物理设计的关键，当前瓶颈在机制重新设计而非参数推断，基准为未来研究提供开放挑战。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve future behavior from interaction experience, yet existing evaluations typically optimize under fixed execution conditions and do not test recovery after those conditions change. To address this gap, we introduce PACE-Bench (Physics Adaptation via Code Evolution), a simulator-grounded benchmark of 144 source-to-target adaptation pairs across six physics domains. Each pair links a source environment to a mutated target environment with the same goal and interface. A code-driven design that succeeds in the source fails in the target, where agents must iteratively adapt it into a working target design using diagnostic sandbox feedback within a limited attempt budget. We compare ten self-evolving methods from four paradigms. The benchmark remains far from saturated: Reflexion + Qwen3-14B succeeds on only 35.9\% of full-benchmark pairs, while GPT-5.5 solves 66.7\% of the Statics subset under the full budget. Together, these results show that simulator-grounded reflection is more reliable than unverified self-revision, while memory anchors agents to early designs and broad tree search explores without converging. Even revealing exact physical changes does not raise the performance ceiling, pointing to mechanism redesign rather than parameter inference as the central bottleneck. Data and code are available at https://github.com/thunlp/PACE-Bench.

</details>


### [140] [SheetCompass: Hierarchical Relation Graphs for Agentic Spreadsheet Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14452)
*Panjing He,Mingyue Cheng,Yucong Luo,Li Li,Xiaohan Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出SheetCompass，一种图引导与记忆驱动的智能体框架，用于处理复杂电子表格推理与自动化任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法将电子表格扁平化为顺序字符串，丢失了工作表内边界和跨表语义，无法利用全局空间上下文，导致推理效果不佳。

Method: SheetCompass显式建模工作表内部及跨工作表的图结构关系，并通过记忆机制维护任务相关信息，增强智能体的推理能力。

Result: 该框架能够更有效地处理复杂工作簿，提升电子表格推理与自动化的性能。

Conclusion: 图引导与记忆驱动的智能体框架可有效弥补LLM在电子表格全局上下文理解上的不足。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used to organize, analyze, and manipulate semi-structured data, yet automated spreadsheet reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs). Real-world workbooks often contain implicit cross-table associations, fine-grained column dependencies, and complex spatial layouts. Existing methods typically flatten these multidimensional structures into sequential strings, losing important intra-sheet boundaries and inter-sheet semantics. Consequently, LLMs cannot exploit the global spatial context that human experts naturally use when inspecting spreadsheets. We propose SheetCompass, a graph-guided and memory-driven agentic framework for spreadsheet reasoning and automation. SheetCompass explicitly models structural relationships within and across worksheets while maintaining task-relevant information in memory, enabling agents to reason more effectively over complex workbooks.

</details>


### [141] [Twin: Playing an Unknown Game with a Test-Time Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14490)
*Alexy Skoutnev,Kirill Acharya,Gaston Longhitano,Madeleine Udell,Kevin Ellis,Iddo Drori*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出测试时世界模型推理系统Twin，通过程序化世界模型和反例修复，在ARC-AGI-3任务中达到97.8%的关卡通过率，远超基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法为每个任务手工设计世界模型，成本高且泛化差；需要一种能从交互中自动学习游戏规则和目标的方法。

Method: Twin系统让编码智能体编写可执行世界模型，用模拟交互构建模型；通过回放验证确保动作前重现所有先前转换，预测与实际不符时用反例修复模型；同时通过搜索或先验推断目标。

Result: 清除179/183关卡（97.8%），效率高于人类在158/179关卡（88.3%）；目标推断在156关卡先于奖励完成；基础模型分数从7.8%提升至93.3%，通关23/25游戏。

Conclusion: 构建可用世界模型比预期简单，真正难点在于正确推断目标；Twin展示了编码智能体+反例驱动修复范式的有效性。

Abstract: We present a Test-time World-model Inference (Twin) system, in which a frontier coding agent writes an executable world model for completing continual learning tasks, such as ARC-AGI-3 games. Traditional approaches hand-engineer such models, one custom design per task. Each game hides its rules and goal, and our system constructs them from simulation and interaction alone. Its inductive prior over grid games is strong enough to recover the true transitions of the game and the goal on nearly all levels. Replay validation happens in a twin world model. The harness enforces that an action is not made until the program reproduces every previous observed game transition. Each mismatch between a world model prediction and the actual action result becomes a counterexample that is used to repair the world model. Twin clears 179 out of 183 levels (97.8%), and does so more efficiently than humans in 158 out of 179 levels (88.3%). The system infers the goal before any reward on 156 of the levels it clears (87.2%), and in the remaining levels automatically discovers the goal by search. The benchmark scores completion and action efficiency, between 0 and 100, against humans playing each game for the first time. Played directly, the base model scores only 7.8%; an off-the-shelf harness increases it to 61.1%, whereas our twin world model increases the same base model to 93.3%, clearing 23 out of 25 games. Building a usable world model is simpler than anticipated, whereas the harder problem is inferring the right goal.

</details>


### [142] [Participatory Moral AI Is Not Neutral: The Invisible Hand of Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14522)
*Taenyun Kim,Edyta Bogucka,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过两个阶段（N=809）的实验，在三个部署情境中系统检验了道德AI elicitation流程中三个关键选择（特征范围、选民抽样、问题表述）对偏好结果的影响，发现每个选择都会显著改变产生的道德偏好，呼吁对该流程进行审计和披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德偏好elicitation方法依赖公众投票训练AI模型，但开发者在投票前做出的三个关键选择（特征范围、选民抽样、问题表述）往往被视为技术细节而被忽视，缺乏系统性研究这些选择如何影响最终道德偏好。

Method: 在两个阶段（总样本N=809）中，针对三个部署情境（AI肾脏分配、AI代理模拟缺席员工、生成式AI描绘逝者），分别操纵特征范围、选民构成（政治意识形态）和问题表述方式，测量参与者的道德偏好及其道德基础关联。

Result: 三个主要发现：1）道德相关特征在不同情境间不转移；2）约三分之一的特征偏好因政治意识形态而异，部分方向相反；3）问题表述可使意识形态差距扩展或缩小最多达一个完整量表点，并改变道德基础与判断的关联方式。

Conclusion: 基于投票的对齐方法仅靠聚合无法实现公平或透明的AI；道德AI elicitation流程的每个阶段都至少应被审计和披露，以确保结果的可信度和伦理性。

Abstract: As AI systems make more morally loaded decisions across society, one response has been moral preference elicitation. In this approach, researchers poll participants on hypothetical dilemmas and use the aggregated votes to train a policy that an AI model then applies at scale. Before any vote is cast, developers make three key choices in the moral AI elicitation pipeline: feature scoping, voter sampling, and question framing. In other words, they decide which features go to a vote, which voters to include, and how to present the question. These choices are often opaque, undocumented, and treated as technical details rather than normative ones. We examine each of these choices within a common empirical study and show that each can shape the preferences produced by moral AI elicitation. Across two phases (N = 809) in three deployment contexts (i.e., AI kidney allocation, AI agents simulating absent workers, and generative AI depictions of the deceased), we examine the three main stages of the moral AI elicitation pipeline. First, morally relevant features shift across contexts. This suggests that feature schemas should not be assumed to transfer across deployment domains. Second, preferences differ by political ideology for roughly one-third of features, with some differences reversing direction. The ideological composition of the voter pool can therefore affect the resulting aggregated preference profile. Third, the wording of the elicitation question can narrow or widen ideological gaps by up to a full scale point. The framing conditions also change how moral foundations are associated with participants' judgments. Taken together, these findings suggest that voting-based alignment cannot deliver fair or transparent AI by aggregation alone; at minimum, each stage of the moral AI elicitation pipeline should be audited and disclosed.

</details>


### [143] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文为LLM应用中的会话交接提供形式化理论，提出以任务相关ICL状态传递为框架，并给出最优交接的存储需求与误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM应用会话交接的理论指导，即当上下文受限或会话重启时需要决定保留哪些信息，以确保任务能高效继续。

Method: 将交接建模为任务相关ICL状态的传递；在外生性条件下，用预测等价性刻画最粗确定性充分交接；提出三部分记录（决策约束、任务统计量、原始观测）并分析线性与非线性回归下的存储与误差关系。

Result: 高斯线性回归给出精确有限维交接及有限位扰动界；非参数回归则建立存储量与均方预测误差间的上下界，量化了先前记忆约束对后续任务的影响。

Conclusion: 提供了判定交接内容的理论与实用方法，并阐明了记忆需求如何随任务特性变化，为实际系统设计提供依据。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Weird Machines in Transport Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13685)
*Michael Collins,Jada Cumberland,Brianne Dunn,Ross Gore,Samuel Jackson,Sachin Shetty,Jonathan Takeshita*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次将怪异机器理论扩展到TLS握手协议，证明OpenSSL和BoringSSL中的合法原语可组合为图灵完备系统，并命名为“信任驱动计算”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前怪异机器研究集中于软件系统和工控网络，但尚未有工作系统分析TLS握手这类与认证决策耦合的协议层计算能力。

Method: 通过形式化定义信任驱动概念，提出TLS实现满足会话存储、序列计数器算术、握手状态条件分支及重传/重试迭代即可构成任意计算条件；并在真实OpenSSL代码路径上构建两个演示（哨兵防护和认证绕过）。

Result: 成功演示了无需内存破坏或外部恶意软件即可利用合法TLS原语实现防御检测和攻击绕过，验证了理论可行性。

Conclusion: TLS握手存在可被利用的潜在计算能力，需将信任驱动纳入安全设计与评估框架。

Abstract: Weird machines are latent computational capabilities that emerge from the composition of architectural components. Prior work has studied this phenomenon extensively in software systems, including x86 instructions, ELF metadata, and page tables, and more recently in cyber-physical systems such as industrial control networks. This paper extends weird machine theory to a new domain: the Transport Layer Security (TLS) handshake and its two dominant implementations, OpenSSL and BoringSSL.
  We show that legitimate TLS primitives, including session cache entries, renegotiation logic, extension parsing, and certificate verification steps, compose into Turing-complete systems whose computation is coupled to authentication and trust decisions rather than physical actuation. We formalize this coupling, which we call trust actuation, and argue that any TLS implementation providing session storage, arithmetic on sequence counters, conditional branching on handshake state, and iteration through resumption or retry loops satisfies the conditions for arbitrary computation.
  We validate this theory with two working demonstrations built on real OpenSSL code paths. The first, a sentinel system, composes standard TLS primitives into a defensive mechanism that detects anomalous handshake behavior. The second, an authentication bypass, composes the same class of primitives into an attack that defeats a cipher-strength policy check through mid-connection renegotiation, without any memory corruption or external malware. Both demonstrations run against real server and client binaries in Docker.

</details>


### [145] [The ack3 H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset: Audit Scope Across 135 Security Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13792)
*Josef Gattermayer,Jan Kalivoda,Arman Bašović*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 基于2026年上半年135起DeFi安全事件的分析，发现68起有审计历史的事件中，67.6%的攻击路径不在审计范围内，且这些路径造成了94.4%的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约审计通常只覆盖特定时间点的特定工件，但“已审计”标签常被误解为整个项目的安全保障。为澄清审计覆盖范围与实际安全事件之间的关系，本研究通过大规模事件数据验证审计范围与事件路径的差异。

Method: 使用ack3发布的H1 2026 DeFi事件数据集，分析135起事件，统计审计历史、攻击路径与审计范围的重叠情况，并按事件数量和损失金额进行加权分析，同时排除极端事件进行稳健性检验。

Result: 68起有审计历史的事件中，46起攻击路径在审计范围外，20起在内，2起未决；范围外路径占事件数量67.6%，占损失94.4%；剔除两起大型事件后，损失占比降至72.1%，但结论方向不变。

Conclusion: 项目级审计历史与攻击路径范围是两个独立变量，审计覆盖并不等同于全面保护；审计范围外的攻击路径是损失的主要来源，时间因素和项目类型也需纳入安全评估。

Abstract: Smart-contract audits cover defined artifacts at a specific time, but the label audited is often treated as project-wide assurance. We analyze audit history and incident-path scope across 135 DeFi security incidents using the H1 2026 DeFi Incident Dataset published by cybersecurity company ack3 (https://ack3.ai), covering 1 January to 29 June 2026. The corpus reports USD 939.86 million in attributed loss. Audit history was identified for 68 incidents. Of these, 46 attack paths were outside all identified public pre-incident audit scopes, 20 were inside at least one scope, and 2 were unresolved. Within this 68-incident subset, outside-scope paths represented 67.6% by count and 94.4% of reported loss. The loss-weighted result was concentrated in two large incidents; excluding both reduced the share to 72.1%, while preserving the direction of the result. We also describe audit age, temporal loss distribution, and affected project types. The results show that project-level audit history and incident-path scope are distinct variables.

</details>


### [146] [Mobile Apps vs. Web Browsers: A User Perception Study with Android Apps and Google Chrome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13803)
*Harel Berger*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验和调查，探讨Android用户在安全、隐私和可用性方面对移动应用与网页浏览器的感知差异，旨在揭示影响用户选择的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对移动应用与网页浏览器的选择受安全、隐私和可用性认知影响，但现有研究缺乏系统性比较，开发者需了解用户关切以优化平台设计。

Method: 结合实验和问卷调查，面向Android智能手机用户收集数据，分析其在安全、隐私和可用性方面的感知与偏好。

Result: 研究识别出影响用户选择的关键担忧和误解，具体结果未在摘要中详述，但指向两平台在用户感知上的显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究为开发者提供了改进移动应用和网页浏览器安全、隐私及可用性的洞见，通过解决用户误解来提升整体体验。

Abstract: This study examines user perceptions of mobile applications (apps) versus web browsers for accessing online services, with an emphasis on security, privacy, and usability aspects. Through a combination of an experiment and a survey with Android smartphone users, the research seeks to identify the key concerns and preferences that influence their choice between mobile apps and web browsers. The findings will offer valuable insights for developers to improve the security, privacy, and usability of both platforms by addressing user concerns and misconceptions.

</details>


### [147] [Vaulted Passkeys: A Device-Bound Proposal for Authenticated Credential Export and Import](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13806)
*Pol Henarejos*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Vaulted Passkeys架构，通过受保护凭据信封实现硬件认证器凭据的可移植备份，避免克隆完整认证器或暴露明文私钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设备绑定凭据的恢复方案依赖预先注册额外认证器，属于冗余而非原始凭据备份，在硬件故障或迁移场景存在恢复缺口。

Method: 采用256位随机Kvault密钥，通过HKDF派生密钥和四种显式AEAD配置保护PKV1凭据信封，分离注册与导出/导入流程，并区分必需保险库与可选身份组件。

Result: 原型验证了可行性，包含角色分离系统模型、线格式、威胁分析、实现映射及可证伪评估计划，但未提供正式安全证明或最终标准。

Conclusion: Vaulted Passkeys在设备绑定凭据可移植性上提供了可行的设计方向，但需进一步形式化验证与标准化工作。

Abstract: Hardware authenticators deliberately resist private-key extraction, yet replacement, disaster recovery, and controlled migration create a legitimate need for portability. Existing guidance for device-bound credentials commonly reduces recovery risk by registering an additional authenticator before failure. That creates an independent credential registration and requires replacement hardware to exist in advance; it is redundancy, not a backup of the original credential. This paper addresses the resulting recovery gap by exporting protected credential state while the source is available and restoring it to hardware acquired later, without cloning a complete authenticator or exposing plaintext private keys to routine desktop software. We propose Vaulted Passkeys, a device-bound architecture in which a random 256-bit Kvault protects authenticated PKV1 credential envelopes through HKDF-separated keys and four explicit AEAD profiles. The design separates enrollment from export/import and the required vault from optional identity. We contribute a role-separated system model, wire format, threat analysis, implementation mapping, and falsifiable evaluation plan. The prototype demonstrates feasibility but is neither a formal security proof nor a proposed final standard.

</details>


### [148] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究XGBoost对垂直异常值和杠杆点的鲁棒性，提出基于M、S和τ估计器的替代损失函数，发现MM-XGBoost两阶段方法在鲁棒性和预测精度间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost虽广泛应用，但其损失函数对异常值敏感，垂直异常值和杠杆点可能严重影响其性能，现有研究未系统探讨此类鲁棒性问题。

Method: 探索将稳健回归中的M、S和τ估计器用作XGBoost的损失函数，并特别设计两阶段的MM-XGBoost流程，以结合高阶稳健性与预测效率。

Result: 实验表明，与标准二次损失和Huber损失相比，MM-XGBoost在数据污染场景下显著提升预测稳健性，同时保持对干净数据的高精度。

Conclusion: MM-XGBoost是增强XGBoost鲁棒性的有效方案，可作为应对异常值场景的默认选择，未来可扩展至其他集成方法。

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [Verified Pythagorean Composition for Adaptive Cryptographic Games: Noise Flooding in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13846)
*Yi Lee,Alexandru Cojocaru,Junyi Liu,Xiaodi Wu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文用Rocq/SSProve机器验证了噪声洪泛对近似同态加密的自适应解密攻击安全性，证明优势上界为β_CPA + √(qn)/(2γ)，并实现了避免线性损失的组合论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声洪泛是对近似同态加密解密攻击的标准防御，但其安全证明对组合敏感：朴素混合论证会随查询次数q线性损失，而密码学证明通过累积条件KL散度并仅做一次转换，获得参数关键的平方根损失。手工证明易错，需要机器验证。

Method: 基于SSProve语义构建新的关系程序逻辑，其Pythagorean判断在不转换为统计距离的情况下组合条件KL预算；开发经过验证的迹编译器，将局部预言机规则提升到任意自适应程序，仅做最终一次转换。

Result: 形式化证明了任意近似正确且IND-CPA安全的FHE方案，对所有q查询IND-CPAD攻击者满足Pr[IND-CPAD_NF^A=1] ≤ β_CPA(B_A,q) + √(qn)/(2γ)，其中n为明文维度，γ为洪泛宽度乘数。

Conclusion: 该工作为噪声洪泛安全性提供了首个机器检查的证明，精确复现了密码学中的平方根损失论证，并展示了关系程序逻辑在组合安全证明中的实用性。

Abstract: Noise flooding is a standard defense against decryption attacks on approximate homomorphic encryption, but its security proof is unusually sensitive to composition. Replacing each of $q$ adaptive decryption answers with a statistically close simulation and applying an ordinary hybrid argument loses linearly in $q$. The cryptographic proof instead accumulates conditional Kullback-Leibler (KL) costs and converts to statistical distance once, giving the parameter-critical square-root loss.
  We machine-check this argument using Rocq and SSProve. Given any fully homomorphic encryption scheme that is approximately correct and IND-CPA secure, we formalize a reduction for every $q$-query IND-CPAD adversary and prove \[
  \Pr[\mathsf{IND\text{-}CPAD}_{\mathsf{NF}}^{\mathcal A}=1]
  \leq β_{\mathsf{CPA}}(\mathcal B_{\mathcal A,q})
  + \frac{\sqrt{qn}}{2γ}. \] where $n$ is the plaintext dimension and $γ$ is the flooding-width multiplier. Our proof constructs a new relational program logic over SSProve semantics. Its Pythagorean judgment composes conditional KL budgets without converting them to statistical distance, and a verified trace compiler lifts a local oracle rule to arbitrary adaptive programs with a single final conversion.

</details>


### [150] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 主动学习在干净标签下能提升学习曲线上的平衡准确率，但其鲁棒性受数据集、噪声结构和评估指标影响，未发现结构性标签噪声的普遍额外惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未清晰区分不确定性采样失败是因获取了更多错误标签，还是因错误集中在难例区域而更具破坏性，本文旨在通过对照实验厘清机制。

Method: 在三个二分类表格数据集上，对比边缘不确定性采样与随机采样，涵盖干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）和有界难度依赖噪声，使用100个配对种子、多种噪声率和预算，并用暴露匹配控制最终错误率，同时进行干净标签扩展至预算400。

Result: 干净标签下不确定性采样在所有数据集上提升平衡准确率曲线下面积1.09-1.77个百分点；难度依赖噪声仅在乳腺癌数据集上部分削弱优势，而暴露匹配分析未显示错误位置带来普遍额外惩罚；在干净MAGIC数据上采样提升平衡准确率但降低平均精度和固定假阳性率下的真阳性率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样总体标签高效，但其表现高度依赖数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标，建议在实际应用中综合多指标评估其效益。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [151] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentao，一个受治理的本地优先运行时，用于工具调用型LLM代理，通过分层架构将模型提议与主机授权执行分离，以增强可治理性、可检查性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理能力增强带来风险，包括过度权限、弱审计性、提示注入、工具投毒和不可控副作用，现有系统缺乏明确的运行时抽象来管理这些风险。

Method: Agentao采用分层架构，包括主机面对接口、主机契约、运行时核心、权限中介工具系统，以及内存、重放、插件、技能、子代理和协议集成等子系统，模型生成动作提议，主机授权执行。

Result: 系统展示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界和执行痕迹作为显式运行时抽象，支持可治理、可检查的代理构建，代码开源。

Conclusion: Agentao不提供正式安全保证，而是提供实用的治理框架，使代理更适合主机控制的本地环境，为构建更可控的LLM代理系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [152] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出KRPRVFL模型，将核风险敏感均值p次幂准则融入RVFL网络，以增强对噪声标签、异常值和类别不平衡的鲁棒性，并通过协作学习机制提升复杂环境下的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL模型对噪声标签、异常值及不平衡数据敏感，限制了其在真实应用中的性能。

Method: 用KRP准则替代最小二乘损失，引入协作学习机制实现组件间自适应交互，并利用核诱导特征映射捕捉非线性关系。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上，KRPRVFL在准确率、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面持续优于基线模型。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL是一种快速、可扩展且可靠的分类解决方案，适用于挑战性任务。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [153] [Characterizing the Variance Envelope: A Multi-Dimensional Analysis of Spectre Telemetry Across Architectures and Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13920)
*Jaya Keshava Chandra Kotha,Jean-Luc Gaudiot*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了Spectre攻击在不同CPU架构、变体、节奏和背景噪声下的硬件性能计数器（HPC）签名变化范围，证明静态检测模型在真实环境中失效，并提出需架构感知的自适应监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的HPC攻击检测在受控环境下有效，但静态模型无法应对真实世界的背景噪声、攻击变体和流量节奏扰动；同时缺乏跨架构（Intel/ARM/AMD）的系统性研究。

Method: 构建覆盖3种攻击变体、4种节奏模式和4种背景噪声条件的实验矩阵，在Intel、ARM、AMD上量化HPC签名偏移的"方差包络"，并分析微架构层面的失败模式。

Result: HPC签名高度脆弱，易受执行环境影响；此外发现AMD Jaguar上Prime+Probe攻击存在持久的硬件级失败瓶颈。

Conclusion: 可靠的运行时攻击检测必须采用架构感知的自适应监控机制，而非静态模型。

Abstract: Hardware attacks like Spectre exploit built-in processor vulnerabilities, leaving anomalous footprints in Hardware Performance Counter (HPC) metrics. While machine learning can detect these footprints in controlled settings, static models fail in the real world when confronted with background system noise, diverse attack variants, and adversarial traffic pacing. To close this gap, this paper characterizes the "variance envelope"-the full range of how attack signatures shift- across Intel, ARM, and AMD architectures. We evaluate an extensive experimental matrix encompassing three attack variants, four pacing modes, and four background-noise conditions. Our analysis proves that HPC signatures are highly fragile and easily warped by their execution environment. Furthermore, we expose a critical microarchitectural bottleneck: the persistent, hardware-level failure of Prime+Probe attacks on the AMD Jaguar. Ultimately, this comprehensive characterization demonstrates that reliable runtime detection requires architecture-aware, adaptive monitoring rather than static models.

</details>


### [154] [CoSA: Context-Aware Severity Assessment via Context Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13928)
*Jinfeng Jiang,Yikun Li,Chengran Yang,Ting Zhang,Wen Bin Leow,Yide Yin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CoSA方法，通过代码属性图与两阶段仓库剪枝（静态+智能体LLM引导）提取漏洞相关上下文，生成度量级摘要并交由轻量Transformer预测CVSS基础指标，在6816个样本上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动评估CVSS基础指标耗时且现有自动化方法忽略仓库级证据，而相关信息分散于整个仓库且噪声大，导致评估不准确。

Method: 构建代码属性图，先进行轻量静态剪枝保留结构邻近上下文，再通过智能体LLM剪枝筛选CVSS相关证据，将检索结果压缩为度量级文本摘要，输入轻量Transformer预测器。

Result: 在真实漏洞实验中，CoSA相比最优基线预测准确率提升14.4%，Macro-F1提升15.3%，并构建了含6816个样本、覆盖90种CWE类型的仓库级数据集。

Conclusion: 显式面向度量指标的仓库上下文检索对自动化严重性评估至关重要，CoSA提供了一种可靠且实用的解决方案，优于函数级和纯LLM方法。

Abstract: Accurate vulnerability severity assessment is essential for prioritizing remediation, yet manually assessing Common Vulnerability Scoring System (CVSS) base metrics remains labor-intensive. Existing automated approaches often fail to capture the repository-level evidence required for assessing many CVSS base metrics. Such repository-aware assessment is challenging because relevant evidence is scattered across the entire repository under heavy noise.
  To address these challenges, we present CoSA, a Context-aware vulnerability Severity Assessment approach that infers CVSS base metrics from repository artifacts. CoSA constructs a code property graph (CPG) and applies a two-stage repository-pruning strategy: lightweight static pruning to preserve structurally proximal context, followed by an agentic large language model (LLM)-guided pruning step to retain CVSS-relevant context while collecting supporting evidence. The LLM then consolidates the retrieved repository context into compact, CVSS metric-wise textual summaries, which are fed into a lightweight transformer predictor. We also construct a higher-quality repository-level dataset comprising 6,816 CVSS labeled instances spanning 90 Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) types.
  Experiments on real-world vulnerabilities show that CoSA consistently outperforms function-level and pure-LLM baselines. It improves prediction accuracy by 14.4% and Macro-F1 by 15.3% over the best-performing baseline, suggesting that explicit, metric-oriented repository context retrieval is crucial for practical and reliable automated severity assessment.

</details>


### [155] [Structural Leakage in Graph Encryption: Attacks and Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13981)
*Hua Shen,Renzhi Chen,Ge Wu,Willy Susilo,Jing Chen,Mingwu Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本报告综合分析了两项关于单对最短路径（SPSP）查询的图加密方案（GES）中的结构泄漏，指出PathGES存在严重不平衡的重轻分解（HLD），导致大量一对一映射，易受Falzon-Paterson攻击和路径长度侧信道推断；提出的Fragment Tree攻击可实现高达10.24%的精确查询恢复；为防御这些攻击，引入BlindGES方案，采用合并与分割机制和两级多映射索引，将一对一映射降至20%以下，设置时间减少50%，存储开销降低32%，路径长度泄漏控制在1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图加密方案（如PathGES）在保护SPSP查询时存在结构泄漏，尤其是HLD分解的不平衡导致大量查询令牌与路径一对一映射，使攻击者能够恢复查询内容和推断路径长度，迫切需要分析这些漏洞并提出更安全的方案。

Method: 首先，对PathGES进行结构泄漏分析，量化HLD分解的不平衡性；其次，提出Fragment Tree攻击利用结构弱点恢复查询；最后，设计BlindGES，采用合并与分割机制重组索引结构，并结合两级多映射索引减少一对一映射，同时提供安全证明和实验验证。

Result: 在七个真实数据集上，Fragment Tree攻击在稀疏图上实现高达10.24%的精确查询恢复；BlindGES将一对一映射比例降至20%以下，设置时间减少50%，存储开销降低32%，路径长度泄漏小于1%。

Conclusion: PathGES的结构不平衡是其主要安全缺陷，Fragment Tree攻击证实了实际威胁；BlindGES通过索引重构和混合机制有效降低结构泄漏，在性能和安全之间取得更好平衡，为未来GES设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Graph encryption schemes (GES) enable secure outsourcing of graph data while supporting efficient queries. This report provides a comprehensive analysis of structural leakage in GES for single-pair shortest path (SPSP) queries, integrating findings from two recent works. First, we analyze PathGES, a scheme designed to resist query recovery attacks through heavy-light decomposition (HLD) and canonical fragment encoding. Our analysis reveals that PathGES suffers from significant imbalances in HLD decomposition, with over 99% of token-path mappings being one-to-one on real-world datasets, enabling both the Falzon-Paterson attack and side-channel inference of path lengths. Second, we present Fragment Tree attack that exploits these structural weaknesses to recover query contents, achieving up to 10.24% exact recovery on sparse graphs. Third, we introduce BlindGES, an enhanced scheme incorporating a Merge-and-Divide mechanism and two-level multimap index that reduces one-to-one mappings to below 20%, cuts setup time by 50%, reduces storage overhead by 32%, and limits path length leakage to under 1%. This report systematically presents attack methodologies, defense mechanisms, security proofs, and experimental evaluations on seven real-world datasets.

</details>


### [156] [BGA: A noise-immune neural distillation framework for malicious signature extraction in high-entropy encrypted flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14126)
*Sheng Hong,Yixuan Huang,Weiwei Jiang,Junyuan Zhang,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijian Jiao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出BGA，一种针对高熵TLS 1.3流量的抗噪神经蒸馏框架，通过ANOVA特征解耦、WGAN-GP数据增强和门控多头注意力机制，在多个基准上达95.2%以上性能，并具备低延迟实时部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵TLS 1.3流量中注意力稀释问题严重，加密噪声掩盖恶意特征，且少数类攻击样本极度不平衡，导致传统检测模型性能下降。

Method: 先用ANOVA解耦高判别性控制平面特征与密码学噪声；再用WGAN-GP（带1-Lipschitz约束）合成少数类样本；核心架构采用BiLSTM提取时序依赖，并创新引入自适应门控多头注意力作为神经滤波器，动态抑制加密伪影、放大恶意签名。

Result: 在CIC-IDS-2018和Edge-IIoT上所有关键指标超95.2%；MSCI攻击召回率提升43.2%；噪声注入测试比vanilla Transformer性能裕度高8.57%；推理延迟0.2820 ms（ARM理论缩放1.6920 ms）。

Conclusion: BGA在精度、鲁棒性和实时性间取得良好平衡，为异构工业边缘网关上的加密威胁检测提供了可行的架构基线，并支持未来硬件实现。

Abstract: To mitigate attention dilution in high-entropy TLS 1.3 flows, we propose BGA, a noise-immune neural distillation framework for encrypted threat intelligence.The methodology first employs Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) to decouple high-discriminatory control-plane features - specifically industrial setpoints - from stochastic cryptographic noise. To resolve the extreme class imbalance within a corpus of 86,878 flow records, a Wasserstein GAN with Gradient Penalty (WGAN-GP) module, enforcing the 1-Lipschitz constraint, is integrated to synthesize high-fidelity minority samples, elevating the detection recall of rare Malicious State Command Injections(MSCI) attacks by 43.2%. At its core, the BGA architecture integrates Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) for temporal dependency extraction and an Adaptive Gated Multi-Head Attention mechanism. This gated unit functions as a neural filter to dynamically suppress encryption artifacts while amplifying malicious signatures. Extensive evaluations on CIC-IDS-2018 and Edge-IIoT benchmarks demonstrate a performance ceiling exceeding 95.2% across all key metrics. Furthermore, noise-injection stress tests confirm BGAs superior structural resilience with a 8.57% performance margin over vanilla Transformers, while its ultra-low inference latency of 0.2820 ms (estimated 1.6920 ms via theoretical scaling for ARM) indicates a high potential for real-time feasibility on heterogeneous industrial edge gateways, providing a promising architectural baseline for future hardware implementation.

</details>


### [157] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标注基准的评估框架，通过强教师模型对学生模型的稀疏纠偏，衡量持续学习系统的相对提升，并验证其与真实性能提升的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准标注在安全场景中稀缺、过时且不具代表性，难以评估LLM+记忆/检索的持续学习系统；LLM裁判和蒸馏方法在有限标注下不可靠。

Method: 基于缩放假设，用更强教师模型提供稀疏高精度纠正，计算学生模型向教师收敛的程度作为评分指标，并验证该指标与独立金标准的相关性。

Result: 在多个安全任务、模型家族和系统设计上，教师相对提升与金标准提升呈正相关；相同量级模型间的LLM裁判无有效信号。

Conclusion: 教师相对提升可作为无标注场景下系统效能的可靠代理，且人类提供类似稀疏纠正时，教师规模模型可通过相同机制进一步提升。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [158] [MazeRunner: Nonlinear Task and Clue Orchestration for LLM-driven Black-Box Automated Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14216)
*Zhenyuan Li,Yi Jiang,Junjie Cheng,Yaokun Li,Jing Qiu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出MazeRunner，一种基于三智能体任务与线索编排框架的自动化渗透测试系统，通过分离全局编排、上下文执行和失败审查，解决真实黑盒测试中攻击图未知、分支探索易迷失、失败误判和长程线索遗忘等问题。在10个HTB目标上，以Claude Sonnet 4.5为后端，完成47.7%的标注子任务，显著优于基线，并实现6个目标的用户级以上访问（含2个root）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的渗透测试代理主要在简化的线性场景中运行端到端流程，而真实黑盒测试本质是非线性的：攻击图初始未知、需从环境反馈增量推断，观察可能揭示多个攻击分支，失败常含歧义，关键线索跨越长行动序列，导致现有代理易陷入深度优先探索、误判失败并遗忘证据。

Method: 构建三智能体任务与线索编排框架（MazeRunner），将全局编排、上下文密集型执行和面向失败的审查分离，并以持久化方式维护任务状态和环境证据，支持行动修订、前提恢复、分支切换和长程线索关联。

Result: 在10个HTB目标上（每个系统限2000万LLM token且防目标特定解法泄漏），MazeRunner完成47.7%的标注子任务，优于PentestGPT-V2（36.2%）和Claude Code（34.2%）；达成6个目标的用户级以上访问（含2个root），两个基线均仅2个目标达用户级且无root；执行轨迹分析显示其探索更多攻击分支并更高效获得shell。

Conclusion: MazeRunner通过显式分离任务编排与执行审查、持久化环境证据，有效应对真实渗透测试的非线性特性，显著提升自动化安全审计的效果与效率，展示了LLM代理在复杂黑盒场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Penetration testing is essential yet resource-intensive. Although large language models (LLMs) show promise for automating security auditing, existing agents mainly execute end-to-end workflows in simplified linear scenarios. Real-world black-box testing is fundamentally nonlinear: the attack graph is initially unknown and must be incrementally inferred from environmental feedback. Observations may reveal multiple attack branches, failures are often ambiguous, and critical clues may span long action horizons. Existing agents therefore tend to become trapped in depth-first exploration, misdiagnose failures, and forget prior evidence. We present MazeRunner, an autonomous penetration testing system built on a three-agent task-and-clue orchestration framework. It separates global orchestration, context-intensive execution, and failure-oriented review while persistently maintaining task states and environmental evidence. This design supports action revision, prerequisite recovery, branch switching, and long-range clue correlation. We evaluate MazeRunner on 10 recently released HTB targets, limiting each system-target run to 20 million LLM tokens and preventing target-specific solution leakage. With Claude Sonnet 4.5, MazeRunner completes 47.7% of annotated subtasks, compared with 36.2% for PentestGPT-V2 and 34.2% for Claude Code. It achieves user-level or higher access on six targets, including root access on two; each same-model baseline reaches user-level access on only two targets and never obtains root access. Execution-trace analysis further shows that MazeRunner explores more attack branches and acquires shells more efficiently.

</details>


### [159] [A Four-Axis Trustworthiness Benchmark for LLM-as-Judge in Principle-Based Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14329)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 发布Principle-Bench基准测试，从准确性、释义鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和校准四个维度评估LLM法官在原则性监管（如英国FCA原则）中的表现；提出Ceca方法实现带校准和反事实归因的评估，发现现有方法无全能胜者，且LLM法官易受关键词堆砌攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原则性监管中的评价标准（如“公平、清晰、不误导”）无法简单二值化，LLM-as-judge正被广泛采用，但缺乏系统性评估框架，尤其在对抗鲁棒性和校准方面未得到充分关注。

Method: 构建包含168个加密资产金融促销场景的Principle-Bench，覆盖两项FCA原则，并设计释义、关键词堆砌和边界扰动三类扰动；提出Ceca（校准的示例聚类评估器），能输出逐示例反事实归因，并与关键词计算、句子嵌入模型、开源LLM法官及校准级联进行对比。

Result: 无方法在所有四个维度上占优；120B LLM法官在良性输入上表现最强，但在关键词堆砌的Consumer Duty输入上准确率从0.74骤降至0.27，且不同模型家庭法官在Cohen's kappa=0.16上一致性极低，表明失败源于模型而非语料。

Conclusion: 部署级LLM法官必须报告按原则划分的对抗欺骗率及事后校准结果，不能仅依赖聚合准确率；Ceca可作为提供可审计归因的候选评估器。

Abstract: Principle-based regulation, with evaluative standards such as "fair, clear, and not misleading" or "deliver good outcomes", cannot be reduced to binary predicates, and LLM-as-judge is increasingly used as the substitute. Our position is that any such judge must be evaluated on four axes: accuracy, paraphrase robustness, adversarial robustness, and calibration. We release Principle-Bench, 168 cryptoasset financial-promotion scenarios mapped to two UK FCA principles, with paraphrase, adversarial keyword-stuffing, and boundary perturbations authored under a pre-registered rubric; the first benchmark covering all four axes for principle-based regulation. We also introduce Ceca (Calibrated Exemplar-Cluster Assessment): a calibrated, auditable assessor that emits exact per-exemplar counterfactual attributions. Across keyword counting, three sentence-transformer embedders, an open-weight LLM-judge, and a calibrated cascade, no method dominates all four axes. A 120B LLM-judge, strongest on benign inputs, loses 47 accuracy points (0.74 to 0.27) on keyword-stuffed Consumer Duty inputs: "compliance theatre." A second judge from a different model family agrees only at Cohen's kappa = 0.16 on that split, localising the failure to the model rather than the corpus. Any deployment-grade LLM-judge for principle-based regulation must report per-principle adversarial deception and post-hoc calibration alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [160] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一个专家引导的神经符号流程，用LLM做语义规范化、模糊推理系统评估脓毒症护理依从性，在MIMIC-IV上揭示抗生素延迟等关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床护理是否遵循循证方案是典型的神经符号问题，但安全关键场景使得纯神经或纯符号方法都不够可靠。

Method: 利用大语言模型严格限制在语义规范化任务，将杂乱药物和微生物字符串映射到固定临床词汇表；再用Sugeno模糊推理系统对规范化事件进行推理，编码八条脓毒症集束化规则并输出[0,1]连续评分。

Result: 在2438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症发作中，发现抗生素时机是最关键缺陷（均值0.24，仅13%在一小时内），Hour-1表现不佳（均值36.7%），51%的高乳酸下降缺口，依从性不同组ICU住院时间有差异（3.8对5.1天）。

Conclusion: 该混合框架能够有效识别临床指南执行中的关键薄弱环节，为安全关键医疗场景提供更细致的依从性评估手段。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [161] [A Hybrid LLM-Based Framework for Automated Security Annotation Generation in Business Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14370)
*Md Kamrul Islam,Tiphaine Henry,Mattia Salnitri,Julius Köpke,Sami Souihi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一个混合框架，结合LLM语义提取、模式约束映射、规则标准化和确定性验证，自动从BPMN模型和安全需求文档生成符合SecBPMN2规范的安全注解，在27个流程模型上验证，显著提升精确率并减少错误注解，同时保持召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BPMN扩展（如SecBPMN2）能支持安全注解，但从自然语言规范推导准确完整的安全注解仍依赖人工，耗时且易错。

Method: 混合框架：先由LLM进行语义提取，再通过模式约束映射、规则基标准化和确定性验证，自动生成符合SecBPMN2的注解。

Result: 生成的注解结构有效且模式完整性高；与人工分析员相比，精确率0.58对0.29，召回率0.52对0.50，错误或错位注解减少近50%，生成速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 混合LLM与规则自动化能降低建模工作量、提高一致性和可靠性，为安全设计BPM提供可扩展基础。
